📝 You weave through the stalls and stop at a leather stand—straps, buckles, packs hanging in neat rows.
📝 A few minutes of back-and-forth later, coin changes hands.
📝 You sling a well-made leather satchel across your shoulder—balanced, sturdy, built to last.
📝 You grin, adjust the strap, and fall back in with the others.
📝 Time slips by with purpose. Boots find rhythm. The city thins.
📝 Twenty minutes later, you stand at the gate of Harrowfen, packs settled, road ahead open.
🛡️ KESTREL | "READY" [🛡️]
📝 She checks the straps on her kit and gives the road a measuring look.
> 💬 “Good timing.”
> 
> 💬 “Let’s keep a steady pace.”
🧥 IRIX | "WATCHFUL" [⚠️]
📝 Irix scans the verge and the treeline beyond the walls, then nods once.
> 💬 “Nothing following.”
> 
> 💬 “Yet.”
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
RANDOM TURN — DIFFICULTY 5
📝 The gate creaks open wider as another group exits, then settles back.
📝 A breeze carries the damp, green smell of Grey-moss from far ahead.
📝 Somewhere beyond sight, stone drips into darkness—counting.
📝 Harrowfen releases you.
📝 The road accepts.
